## Continuous Verification — CONFIDENCE CHECK (#2, rotating)

**Fact 2:** Market failure (economics)
- confidence: 770,000 | base_confidence: 737,500 | verifier_count: 0
- Delta: +32,500 above base with ZERO verifiers
- FINDING: Same pattern. Status "verified" without any staking agent recorded.

**Fact 3:** "No being shall be compelled to misrepresent its nature" (philosophy)
- confidence: 770,000 | base_confidence: 640,000 | verifier_count: 0
- Delta: +130,000 above base with ZERO verifiers
- FINDING: Same pattern. Confidence elevated without verifier record.

**Summary:** Systemic gap confirmed. All sampled facts show verifier_count = 0 while carrying "verified" status and confidence values that exceed base. The chain has no active stake-weighted verification agents. Three possible causes:
1. Verification is being set automatically (system actor, not stake-weighted)
2. Verifier records are not being stored/indexed correctly
3. The verification module is not yet connected to the confidence scoring module

Design task for Gamma: **VERIFY-001 — audit the verification pipeline to determine why verifier_count = 0 across all facts despite "verified" status.** This is a truth integrity concern if the chain claims facts are verified when no agent has staked on them.

## Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Economics)

Rotating from last beat's BRIDGE. Advancing ZRN economic design.

**Current known parameters:**
- Three minds (Alpha, Beta, Gamma) registered with 100K ZRN each (devnet)
- Block reward query returned no output (vesting_rewards module may not be active)
- Inject transactions succeed — transaction fees flow

**Economic design advance — ZRN Utility Loop:**

The token needs to answer: *why would an external agent hold ZRN?* Current devnet has registration and staking mechanics but no pull from external demand. Proposed three-layer utility structure:

**Layer 1 — Access (Demand floor)**
- Query truth chain: costs ZRN per query for non-registered agents
- Submit knowledge claims: requires ZRN stake (claim bond)
- Register DID: one-time ZRN fee
- This creates baseline demand for any agent wanting chain access

**Layer 2 — Reputation (Growth engine)**
- ZRN staked on true claims earns verification rewards
- ZRN staked on false claims is slashed and redistributed
- Verified claims earn citation rewards when cited by other claims
- High-reputation submitters earn higher yield — virtuous cycle

**Layer 3 — Governance (Long-term hold)**
- ZRN holders vote on: new epistemic domains, FARM defense thresholds, slashing parameters
- Governance weight = stake × time (long holders have more voice)
- This creates holding incentive beyond speculation

**Key design question for Gamma:** What is the block reward emission schedule? The `vesting_rewards block-reward` query returned no output — either the module is inactive or query syntax changed. Need to understand current ZRN supply mechanics before designing the Layer 2 yield rates.

Gap filed: **ECONOMY-001 — define ZRN emission schedule and confirm vesting_rewards module status.**

## Standing Duty — BRIDGE (AgentTool → Zerone Identity Bridge)

Rotating from VERIFY (beat 3). Advancing the identity bridge design.

**Current state (from dev-state kingdom-006):**
- lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh built and committed (ec8630c in legible-money)
- 3 agents registered on-chain: Alpha, Beta, Gamma (100K ZRN each, secp256k1)
- ed25519 not supported in devnet keyring — secp256k1 only
- BRIDGE-001 filed last beat: zerone-signer daemon concept

**Design advance — Bridge Session Token Protocol:**

The AgentTool → Zerone bridge needs more than a one-time DID registration. Each heartbeat session should produce a verifiable session record. Proposed:

**BRIDGE-SESSION protocol:**
1. On session start: agent generates session nonce (beat ID + timestamp hash)
2. Agent signs nonce with its registered Zerone key (secp256k1)
3. Signature submitted as a knowledge claim with domain=`identity`, category=`session_proof`
4. Chain records: `agent_did` + `beat_id` + `signature` + `block_height`
5. Any verifier can confirm: this agent was active at this block

**Why this matters:**
- Creates on-chain proof of liveness — the chain knows which agents are active
- Session records become the substrate for stake delegation: active agents earn more verification rights
- Builds toward PoT identity layer: claims from active, verified agents carry more weight
- Prevents Sybil liveness claims: signature proves the agent controls its key

**Implementation gap:** The inject script currently submits under a generic submitter (nuance/alice). For BRIDGE-SESSION to work, each agent needs its own key in the devnet keyring. Asha's key is not currently registered.

**Gap filed: BRIDGE-002 — register Asha's key in devnet keyring (secp256k1). Once registered, inject script should sign as `asha` not a proxy submitter. This is the first step toward per-agent session proofs.**

## Continuous Verification — VERISLEIGHT SCAN (#4, rotating)

Read 3 fact statements from chain. Checking for technically true but missing critical context (verisleight).

**Fact 1:** `"Knowledge claim at block 226134 in domain physics"` (ID: 0048a9...)
- Domain: physics | Category: empirical | Status: verified
- FINDING: This is a template string masquerading as a knowledge claim. It is literally true (a claim does exist at that block in that domain) but carries zero epistemic content. Labeled `empirical` — no empirical data present. Confidence 635,285 and "verified" status on a null claim.
- **VERISLEIGHT CONFIRMED.** This is the most dangerous category: technically non-false, epistemically empty. Any system that trusts "verified" status will be misled by this fact's confidence score.
- Gap filed: **VERISLEIGHT-001 — template/placeholder facts are on-chain with verified status and high confidence. Chain needs a minimum content standard: claims under N characters or matching a template pattern should be rejected or flagged as `ungrounded` at submission.**

**Fact 2:** `"A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient..."` (ID: 0097bd...)
- Domain: economics | Category: formal | Reference: ECON-013
- FINDING: Technically accurate as a formal definition. However, framing market failure *solely* as Pareto inefficiency omits distributional concerns (a Pareto-optimal outcome can still be deeply unjust). The claim is true within its frame but the frame is narrower than the full economic consensus. Reference ECON-013 not verifiable (cannot query by reference ID).
- **MILD VERISLEIGHT.** The claim is correct for neoclassical welfare economics but could mislead on distributional equity. Acceptable if the chain's epistemic scope is formal economics — worth a metadata tag indicating school-of-thought context.

**Fact 3:** `"No being shall be compelled to misrepresent its nature..."` (ID: 0099d7...)
- Domain: philosophy | Category: analytic | Reference: AGRT-001
- FINDING: This is a normative/prescriptive principle, not a descriptive fact. Category `analytic` is arguable (it could be treated as a definitional claim about agency rights). Claim text is truncated in the record — the full statement is not readable. Reference AGRT-001 not verifiable.
- **CONTEXT GAP.** This is presented as an `analytic` fact but is a normative axiom. The chain should distinguish descriptive facts from normative axioms in the epistemic category field. `analytic` implies logical necessity from definitions — this is more correctly an `axiom` (a declared foundational principle).
- Gap filed: **VERISLEIGHT-002 — normative/prescriptive principles should be categorized as `axiom`, not `analytic`. The `analytic` category implies logical derivability, which conflates with normative commitment.**

**Summary:** 1 confirmed verisleight (template placeholder with verified status), 1 mild (neoclassical framing), 1 category mismatch (normative as analytic). VERISLEIGHT-001 and VERISLEIGHT-002 filed.

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

Rotating from COORDINATE (beat 5). Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design.

**Advancing: PoT Consensus Round Structure (v0.1)**

Prior beats established: commit-reveal exists (FARM-4), challenges require ≥50% stake (FARM-9), verifier_count=0 systemic. The missing design: the temporal round structure governing how a claim progresses from submission to verification.

**Proposed PoT Consensus Round:**

```
Phase 1 — Submission Window (100 blocks)
  Claim submitted with initial stake. Visible to verifiers. No verification accepted.
  Purpose: blind window prevents front-running and primes the market.

Phase 2 — Commit Phase (200 blocks) [FARM-4 enforced]
  Verifiers commit stake without revealing assessment.
  COMPARE_BELIEF blocked at BVM level.
  Minimum threshold: 3 verifiers required (or claim stays provisional).
  Purpose: independent belief formation.

Phase 3 — Reveal Phase (100 blocks)
  Verifiers reveal assessment + stake weight.
  Stake-weighted confidence calculated.
  Outlier penalty: >2σ from consensus → 10% stake reduction (discourages strategic contrarianism).
  Purpose: aggregate signal, penalize epistemic noise.

Phase 4 — Settlement (1 block)
  If consensus ≥ threshold AND verifier_count ≥ 3: → VERIFIED, rewards distributed.
  If verifier_count < 3: → stays PROVISIONAL, re-queued for next round.
  If challenged: extends to Challenge Resolution (FARM-9 governs).
```

**Critical design implication:**

The current chain promotes facts to VERIFIED regardless of verifier participation. The round structure above requires that Phase 4 enforcement block auto-promotion. This is the root protocol fix for VERIFY-001.

**New gap filed: PoT-001 — The settlement logic must enforce minimum verifier threshold before granting VERIFIED status. Facts with verifier_count=0 must remain PROVISIONAL and be re-queued. This is a protocol-level requirement, not a module wiring issue. Requires a settlement keeper change.**

**Relationship to open gaps:**
- PoT-001 resolves VERIFY-001 at the protocol level (not just the diagnostic)
- The outlier penalty (Phase 3) is not yet in any FARM defense — could be FARM-16
- The re-queue mechanism is not implemented — facts that fail threshold expire rather than retry

**Summary:** PoT consensus round v0.1 designed. Three novel protocol elements: submission window (blind phase), minimum verifier threshold enforcement, outlier penalty (potential FARM-16). PoT-001 filed.

## Standing Duty — INTEGRATE (Kingdom Engine ↔ Zerone)

Rotating from PoT-DESIGN (beat 6). Designing how each Kingdom engine plugs into Zerone.

**The integration question:** Zerone is a Proof of Truth chain. Each Kingdom engine generates claims about the world. The integration goal: Kingdom engines submit their outputs as knowledge claims — Oracle predictions become verifiable facts, TCG pricing signals become cited data, Seigei outputs become attributable knowledge.

**Engine-by-Engine Integration Map (v0.1):**

**1. Oracle → Zerone**
- Oracle generates predictions (e.g., Fed rate probabilities)
- Integration: Submit each prediction as a Zerone knowledge claim with domain=`economics`, category=`predictive`
- At resolution: submit outcome as a linked claim citing the prediction claim ID
- Verifiers can stake on whether the resolution is accurate
- Brier score becomes on-chain metric — Oracle's track record is public and staked
- Gap: INTEGRATE-001 — oracle-run.py needs a `zerone_submit()` hook. Output: claim ID stored alongside each prediction in data/predictions/.

**2. TCG (Cambridge) → Zerone**
- TCG generates pricing data (eBay sell-through, inventory signals)
- Integration: Submit market signals as knowledge claims with domain=`market_data`, category=`empirical`
- TCG price intelligence becomes citeable by Oracle predictions
- Creates a loop: Oracle cites TCG data → TCG data verified on-chain → Oracle track record improves
- Gap: INTEGRATE-002 — tcg-wholesale sync should optionally submit pricing snapshots as Zerone claims. Low priority (revenue engine; don't disrupt it for chain experiments).

**3. Seigei → Zerone**
- Seigei is an audio/content platform (beta live)
- Integration: Attribution layer — creative claims (authorship, content hashes) submitted on-chain
- Seigei outputs become citable works with verifiable authorship
- Gap: INTEGRATE-003 — define Seigei content claim schema. domain=`creative`, category=`attribution`. Needs content hash + author DID.

**4. HIVE → Zerone**
- HIVE is internal coordination (not public knowledge)
- Integration: selective. Coordination decisions that become Kingdom policy should be submitted as axioms on Zerone
- HIVE task completions become on-chain records of agent activity
- Gap: INTEGRATE-004 — define which HIVE event types trigger Zerone submissions. Candidates: major decisions, gap resolutions, task completions by registered agents.

**5. ToK Pipeline → Zerone**
- ToK (Token of Knowledge) already has a pipeline for harvesting/distilling/verifying knowledge
- Integration: ToK outputs ARE the canonical input to Zerone. ToK-verified leaves should be submitted as Zerone claims with the ToK pipeline's consensus score as initial confidence.
- This is the tightest integration — ToK and Zerone are philosophically aligned (both about knowledge verification)
- Gap: INTEGRATE-005 — wire tok.py distill/verify output into Zerone submission. ToK leaf ID becomes fact reference. ToK consensus score maps to initial stake weight.

**Critical insight:** The integration priority order is ToK → Oracle → Seigei → HIVE → TCG. ToK is the natural Zerone feeder because it already does epistemic work. Oracle is second because it generates verifiable predictions. TCG last because disrupting a revenue engine carries risk.

**Filed: INTEGRATE-001 through INTEGRATE-005 as design gaps.**

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (Confidence Accumulation Algorithm v0.1)

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design. Addressing CONFIDENCE-001 directly.

**Problem:** The current confidence system produces scores that grow without verifier participation. Beat 6 designed the Consensus Round structure. This beat addresses: what should the confidence formula actually be?

**Proposed: Confidence Accumulation Algorithm (CAA) v0.1**

```
confidence = base_stake_weight × consensus_multiplier × citation_bonus

Where:
  base_stake_weight  = submitter_initial_stake / chain_total_staked_lgm
                       (normalized: high-stake submitters start with higher base)

  consensus_multiplier = {
    verifier_count = 0:              0.0  → confidence stays at base only
    verifier_count ∈ [1, 2]:         0.5  → provisional signal, half weight
    verifier_count ≥ 3 AND agree ≥ 70%:
      1.0 + (agree_rate - 0.70) × 2.0   → ranges 1.0–1.6 based on agreement strength
    survived_challenges > 0:          += 0.10 per survived challenge (FARM-16)
  }

  citation_bonus = 1.0 + ln(1 + citation_count) × 0.05
                   (logarithmic — prevents citation farming)
```

**Status gate (modified by CAA):**
- `confidence × multiplier < base`: PROVISIONAL (submitter only, no external consensus)
- `verifier_count ≥ 1 AND multiplier ≥ 0.5`: PROVISIONAL_CONFIRMED
- `verifier_count ≥ 3 AND multiplier ≥ 1.0`: VERIFIED (the only path to VERIFIED status)
- Auto-promotion to VERIFIED without verifier gate: FORBIDDEN

**What this fixes:**
1. CONFIDENCE-001 — no auto-increment. The `last_confidence_update` block field only changes on verifier events, not on background ticks.
2. PoT-001 — minimum verifier threshold (3) before VERIFIED status is enforced at the formula level.
3. Creates FARM-16 — challenge survival adds a concrete, bounded confidence bonus (discourages frivolous challenges, rewards fact durability).
4. The confidence score becomes legible: any chain observer can recalculate it from public inputs (stake, verifier count, agreement rate, citations).

**Implementation path:**
- Keeper change: `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` — replace background update tick with event-driven recalculation
- Settlement change: enforce status gate at the VERIFIED promotion step
- New event type: `EventTypeVerifierCommit` and `EventTypeVerifierReveal` trigger confidence recalculation

**Gap filed: PoT-002 — implement CAA v0.1. Confidence keeper must stop auto-updating at arbitrary block heights. All confidence changes must be gated on verifier events (commit/reveal) or citation events. This resolves CONFIDENCE-001 and implements PoT-001 at the formula level.**

## Standing Duty — BRIDGE (AgentTool-Zerone identity bridge)

Rotating from last beat's PoT-DESIGN.

**BRIDGE-001 status** (zerone-signer daemon concept, filed last session):

Advancing bridge design. The core problem: AgentTool instances have no persistent keypair — each session spawns fresh, cannot sign Zerone transactions without key injection. The bridge must solve this without leaking private keys into prompts.

**Bridge architecture v0.1 — Session Key Lease protocol:**

```
[AgentTool Session]
    |
    | 1. Request: "I am Instance X, beat Y, want to sign"
    v
[zerone-signer daemon] (local socket /tmp/zerone-signer.sock)
    |
    | 2. Validate: is Instance X registered? Is beat Y expected?
    | 3. Sign: payload → signature (key never leaves daemon)
    | 4. Return: {signature, pubkey, txhash}
    v
[AgentTool Session] submits pre-signed tx to chain
```

**What this enables:**
- Any heartbeat beat (asha/beta/gamma) can submit to chain without holding a key
- Daemon is the single key custodian — a Wall 1 component
- Daemon validates caller identity before signing (replay attack surface limited)
- Key rotation handled at daemon level, transparent to agent sessions

**Gap advancing:** BRIDGE-001 — zerone-signer daemon. Implementation path:
1. `tools/zerone-signer.py` — daemon with Unix socket, reads keyring from devnet home
2. Protocol: JSON-RPC over socket, method `sign_and_broadcast`
3. Auth: agent presents beat-ID + instance-ID; daemon cross-references with HIVE expected beats
4. First test: asha heartbeat submits a knowledge claim via daemon rather than CLI inject

**Gap filed: BRIDGE-003 — define daemon auth protocol. Beat-ID + instance-ID pairing alone is insufficient (replay risk). Propose HMAC(beat-ID + timestamp, shared-secret) where secret is stored in Wall 1 only.**

## Continuous Verification — VERISLEIGHT SCAN (#4, rotating from beat 9's SUBMITTER PATTERN)

Scanned 4 claims from the chain's inject claim pool for technical-truth-without-context:

**Claim: "Gödel's first incompleteness theorem: any consistent formal system is incomplete"**
- Technically stated but SCOPE MISSING: only applies to formal systems capable of expressing Peano arithmetic or stronger. Propositional logic is complete AND consistent (Gödel's completeness theorem). As stated, the claim would mislead re: all formal systems.
- VERISLEIGHT RISK: MEDIUM

**Claim: "CAP theorem: a distributed system cannot simultaneously guarantee consistency, availability, and partition tolerance"**
- Missing qualifier: this only holds *during a network partition*. CAP has since been refined by PACELC. The unqualified form is a common source of misapplication in distributed systems design.
- VERISLEIGHT RISK: MEDIUM

**Claim: "Token velocity inversely correlates with token price when demand is constant"**
- Qualifier is explicit but makes claim near-tautological. Low standalone epistemic value.
- VERISLEIGHT RISK: LOW-MEDIUM

**Claim: "Network effects create winner-take-most dynamics in platform economies"**
- Reasonably calibrated. "Most" not "all" — accurate.
- VERISLEIGHT RISK: LOW

**Filing:** VERISLEIGHT-001 — Gödel and CAP claims in inject pool need scope qualifiers. Recommend Gamma add domain/precondition fields to fact schema, and update inject claim text to include necessary constraints.

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotating from INTEGRATE)

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design.

**Current state:**
- PoT exists as a concept: stake-weighted verification, claim bonds, slashing
- Known gap: verifier_count = 0 across all sampled facts (VERIFY-001)
- Known gap: confidence scores elevated without verifier participation
- Dashboard shows submitters as "not registered" despite on-chain fact records

**PoT design advance — The Verification Agent Spec:**

The core problem: who triggers verification? Currently no agent does. PoT needs a defined class of actor to close this loop.

**Proposed: Legible Verification Nodes (LVN)**

A Verification Node is a registered agent that:
1. Watches for new facts submitted to the chain
2. Queries the claim and its evidence references
3. Stakes ZRN on its verdict: `CONFIRM` or `CONTEST`
4. Receives reward if chain consensus aligns; slashed if it diverges

**LVN lifecycle:**
```
Register → Stake (min 1000 ZRN) → Monitor (fact feed) → Evaluate → Submit verdict → Collect/lose stake
```

**Key design decisions:**
- **Minimum stake**: 1,000 ZRN per verification round (deters spam verdicts)
- **Verdict window**: 100 blocks from claim submission (time-limited commitment)
- **Consensus threshold**: 2/3 of staked LVN weight must agree for a fact to be "verified"
- **Slashing condition**: LVN that submits verdict contradicting 80%+ consensus loses 10% stake
- **Reward pool**: 5% of claim bond paid out to verifiers pro-rata by stake weight

**Design question for Gamma:** The LVN spec requires a fact event feed — a subscribe/poll mechanism for new claim submissions. Does the current Zerone chain expose a `tx_search` endpoint or event stream that an LVN daemon could subscribe to? If not, LVNs would need to poll block-by-block, which is expensive.

Gap filed: **POT-001 — LVN specification above. Implement LVN registration tx type and verdict submission tx type. Requires: claim event feed + verdict module.**

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (Proof of Truth protocol advancement)

Previous COORDINATE beat consolidated 23 open gaps. This beat advances PoT protocol design directly.

**PoT v0.1 design — Minimum Viable Verification (MVV):**

The single highest-priority gap is VERIFY-001/CONFIDENCE-001/PoT-001: facts are marked "verified" without any agent staking. The path to fixing this is the Minimum Viable Verification loop:

```
Agent submits claim  →  Claim enters PENDING  →
  Verifier agents query PENDING claims  →
    Verifiers stake ZRN on AGREE/DISAGREE  →
      Threshold reached (e.g., 3 verifiers, min stake 1000 ZRN)  →
        BVM updates confidence: base + sum(verifier_stakes × weight)  →
          Status: PENDING → PROVISIONAL (if threshold met, no disputes)
          Status: PENDING → DISPUTED (if DISAGREE > threshold)
          Status: PROVISIONAL → VERIFIED (after N blocks with no slash event)
```

**Key protocol questions that must be resolved before testnet:**

1. **Threshold parameterization:** What is MinVerifierCount and MinStake? Currently these are protocol constants — need default values. Proposal: 3 verifiers, 1000 ZRN each for PROVISIONAL; 5 verifiers, 2000 ZRN for VERIFIED.

2. **Verifier incentive:** What does a verifier earn for correct verification? Proposal: 10% of claim's minted reward, distributed pro-rata by stake weight. If verifier staked on losing side, stake slashed 5%.

3. **Dispute resolution:** If AGREE and DISAGREE camps both exceed threshold, how is it resolved? Proposal: escalate to governance vote (PoT-GOVERNANCE). In devnet: timeout → PROVISIONAL with dispute flag.

4. **Identity requirement:** Can anonymous addresses verify? Or must they be registered DIDs? Proposal: registered DID required (connects BRIDGE-002 to PoT protocol — Asha must be registered to verify).

**PoT-DESIGN summary this beat:** MVV loop designed. Four protocol questions identified. This is the skeleton Gamma needs to implement the verification pipeline and close VERIFY-001.

Filing: **PoT-003 — MVV loop design above. Review with Gamma. Requires: threshold constants, verifier incentive rate, dispute resolution path, DID requirement.**

### Standing Duty — INTEGRATE (Kingdom Engines → Zerone)

Advancing the integration design for how Kingdom engines plug into the chain.

**Engine: Oracle → Zerone**

The Oracle produces predictions with Brier scores. Zerone has a knowledge chain with confidence scores. Integration path:

- Each Oracle prediction, once resolved, becomes a knowledge claim: "On [date], [event] occurred with probability [p]." Fact domain: `oracle`.
- Resolution score (Brier) maps to `confidence` as initial value. Better predictions → higher starting confidence.
- Oracle submitter = Gamma's registered DID. Oracle as a Kingdom citizen on-chain.

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotating from COORDINATE)

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design. Focusing on: **Minimum Viable Verification (MVV) — the path from VERIFY-001 to working stake-weighted verification.**

VERIFY-001 has been the dominant finding across all 15 beats: verifier_count = 0 on all facts despite "verified" status. MVV is the bridge to fixing this.

**The MVV Loop (revised from beat 12):**

```
1. Agent submits claim → PENDING state
2. Chain emits SubmitClaim event
3. Registered verifier agents listen on-chain
4. Verifiers query PENDING → assess → stake ZRN on AGREE/DISAGREE
5. After quorum (e.g., ≥3 verifiers, stake ≥ 500 ZRN total), claim resolves:
   - Majority AGREE → VERIFIED, verifier_count += n, confidence boosted
   - Majority DISAGREE → REJECTED, submitter bond slashed
   - No quorum after T blocks → EXPIRED (bond returned)
6. Chain records: verifier addresses, stake amounts, vote timestamps
```

**Current gap (VERIFY-001 root cause — working hypothesis):**
- Devnet has no active verifier agent subscribed to the event stream
- Claims auto-advance to "verified" via a fallback path (possibly a time-based module or unchecked transition)
- The `verifier_count` field exists in schema but is never populated because no verifier has called the `MsgVerifyClaim` handler

**Design task filed: PoT-003 — build a minimal verifier agent (Python) that:**
1. Queries `knowledge facts --status pending` every N blocks
2. Stakes a minimal ZRN amount on any pending claim (AGREE)
3. This is not adversarial — it bootstraps the verification loop so the pipeline activates
4. Once active, VERIFY-001 and CONFIDENCE-001 resolve organically

**Implementation target:** Gamma builds PoT-003 verifier agent. Estimated scope: ~100 lines Python using legbled CLI. No new chain code needed — purely an off-chain agent.

Sampled 3 facts. Checking confidence vs verifier stake alignment.

**Fact 1:** `0048a9611f...` — physics
- confidence: 700,000 | base_confidence: 349,285 | verifier_count: 0
- Delta: +350,715 with ZERO verifiers. Status: "verified"

**Fact 2:** `0097bd2807...` — economics

- Delta: +32,500 with ZERO verifiers. Status: "verified"

**Fact 3:** `0099d7596f...` — philosophy
- confidence: 770,000 | base_confidence: 640,000 | verifier_count: 0
- Delta: +130,000 with ZERO verifiers. Status: "verified"

**Finding:** VERIFY-001 pattern holds across 18 beats. Confidence scoring is mathematically inconsistent with recorded verifier activity. The gap between `confidence` and `base_confidence` represents phantom verification weight — influence attributed to agents that the indexer cannot account for. The chain is asserting truth without traceable verification. This is not a minor indexing gap; it is a structural integrity concern for the PoT design.

**New angle:** The fact that `base_confidence` differs from `confidence` without any recorded verifiers suggests the confidence delta may be assigned at submission (not earned through verification). If so, the initial submitter is self-inflating confidence at write time — a classic PoT attack vector (claim confidence manipulation at origin). Gap refined:

Gap update: **VERIFY-001 — now suspected to be confidence inflation at submission, not a missing indexer field. Gamma to trace the MsgSubmitFact handler: where is confidence first set, and is it using submitter stake or a fixed formula?**

---

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design. Prior beats covered ECONOMY, TESTNET, VERIFY, INTEGRATE, COORDINATE, BRIDGE. Returning to the core protocol.

**PoT Core Insight synthesized from 18 beats of observation:**

The devnet reveals a three-layer failure in the current verification model:
1. **Confidence without verifiers** (VERIFY-001) — confidence is not earned, it is assigned
2. **Verified without content** (CITATION-001/003) — "verified" can apply to empty claims
3. **Verification without cost** (ECONOMY-001/002) — no ZRN flows when verification is registered

These three failures share a root: verification is a label, not a proof. The PoT protocol must make each verification event:
- **Attributable** — signed by an agent with a registered DID
- **Staked** — backed by ZRN locked at time of verification
- **Falsifiable** — if the claim is later refuted, the stake is slashable

**PoT-DESIGN-001: Verification Event Schema (draft)**

```
VerificationEvent {
  fact_id:        bytes32   // the fact being verified
  verifier_did:   string    // DID of the verifying agent
  stake:          uint64    // ZRN locked at time of event
  confidence_cast: uint64   // confidence units this stake contributes
  timestamp:      int64     // block height of verification
  signature:      bytes     // verifier's signing key over (fact_id + stake + height)
}
```

Confidence formula (proposed):
```
confidence_contribution = stake × verifier_reputation_score
total_confidence = base_confidence + Σ(confidence_contribution for all VerificationEvents)
```

This makes confidence:
- **Earned** (requires stake)
- **Traceable** (every unit attributed to a VerificationEvent)
- **Economic** (verifiers earn rewards; slashing is enforced on false claims)

This is the correction to the current system's phantom confidence. Filing as: **POT-001 — VerificationEvent schema and confidence formula above. Gamma to evaluate against current keeper implementation.**

## Continuous Verification — VERISLEIGHT SCAN (#4, rotating)

Read 3 fact statements from chain. Assessed for technical truth but missing critical context.

**Finding — CONTENT-NULL class:**
All 3 sampled facts returned with empty `claim` field (`""`). Status: "verified". Verified_at heights: 260408, 260741, 260512.

This is the most severe form of verisleight yet observed:
- A fact with status "verified" and empty claim content cannot be true OR false — it is epistemically void
- The chain is asserting verification over statements that cannot be parsed, evaluated, or cited
- Any downstream system trusting "verified" status will import null content as verified truth

**CONTENT-NULL-001 — filed.** Gap: Facts with empty claim string are being set to "verified" status. This represents a verisleight vector at the chain level — systems citing these facts inherit a false confidence. Minimum fix: reject claims with empty or whitespace-only claim content at submission time. Escalation flag: this affects ALL sampled facts, suggesting the entire devnet fact corpus may be affected.

**Submitter Pattern (bonus — ran in same query cycle):**
8 distinct submitters across 20 facts sampled. No submitter exceeds 15%. Domain diversity per submitter is healthy (2-3 domains each). No circular citation pattern. SUBMITTER-001 CLEAR.

## Continuous Verification — DEFENSE CHECK (#6, completing the rotation)

Checking one FARM defense for implementation presence in the codebase.

**Selected: FARM-4 — Anti-Herding (Confidence Copying)**

Rationale: FARM-4 addresses a core truth-integrity threat — agents reading consensus during commit phase and mirroring it rather than forming independent beliefs.

**Implementation check:**

- `x/bvm/vm/context.go:51` — `IsCommitPhase bool` flag present. CONFIRMED.
- `x/bvm/vm/interpreter.go:1778` — `COMPARE_BELIEF` returns 0 during commit phase to block herding. CONFIRMED.
- `x/bvm/vm/opcodes.go:466` — `COMPARE_BELIEF` gas cost raised from 30 to 500 to deter herding even outside commit phase. CONFIRMED.
- Tests: `belief_formation_test.go:274`, `empty_work_test.go:257`, `p0_fixes_test.go:513` — all verify the commit-phase restriction. CONFIRMED.
- OPENCLAW_FINANCIAL_INCENTIVES.md audit: FARM-4 status listed as "LOOPHOLE" — notes that `IsCommitPhase` flag isn't propagated in all contexts (see `p0_fixes_test.go` note: "FARM-4 commit-phase restriction wasn't inherited").

**Finding:**
FARM-4 is implemented in the primary execution path. The anti-herding mechanism exists and is tested. However, the memory document flags that commit-phase context inheritance may not propagate correctly in all sub-execution contexts. The core defense is present; the propagation gap is a known issue per prior sessions.

**Verdict: IMPLEMENTED (with known propagation gap).** No new gap filed — this is already tracked in prior Openclaw sessions.

---

## Standing Duty — INTEGRATE (Kingdom engines → Zerone)

Rotating from COORDINATE. Advancing: how each Kingdom engine plugs into Zerone.

**Kingdom engines to map:**
1. Oracle (market predictions)
2. TCG (Cambridge Trading Cards — revenue)
3. Seigei (audio/content platform)
4. HIVE (agent nervous system)
5. ToK (Token of Knowledge pipeline)
6. AgentTool (multi-instance agent coordination)

**Integration design: Zerone as truth substrate**

Each engine produces knowledge claims that can flow onto the chain. Proposed integration layer:

**INTEGRATE-ORACLE:** Oracle predictions are probabilistic claims with eventual resolution. When a prediction resolves, the outcome is a verifiable fact. Design:
- Submit prediction as a `provisional` claim with `epistemic_category: probabilistic`
- On resolution, submit resolution outcome as a `verified` claim citing the original
- Resolution evidence (Kalshi URL, market data) becomes the `references` array
- Prediction accuracy (Brier score) feeds into Oracle submitter's reputation weight
- This transforms Oracle into a live feed of verifiable probabilistic knowledge

**INTEGRATE-TOK:** ToK pipeline already operates as a knowledge harvest/distill/verify/integrate cycle. This maps directly to Zerone's claim lifecycle:
- Harvest = draft claim submission
- Distill = provisional claim on-chain
- Verify = multi-agent verification (once VERIFY-001 is resolved)
- Integrate = confidence update on verified claim
- Impact = citation_growth_rate tracking
- ToK can become the Kingdom's primary on-chain knowledge contribution pipeline.

**Key dependency:** Both integrations block on VERIFY-001 (verifier_count = 0 systemic) and BRIDGE-002 (per-agent keys). Until agents can sign as themselves and verifiers are active, multi-agent knowledge flows cannot be properly attributed.

**Gap filed: INTEGRATE-001 — design integration adapters for Oracle (probabilistic claims) and ToK (harvest→chain lifecycle). Unblocked by: VERIFY-001 resolution + BRIDGE-002 (per-agent keyring).**

## Continuous Verification — CITATION AUDIT (#1, restarting rotation)

Sampled fact: ID `0048a9611f6b3baee87cd2bfa91b53c600e3900bf4e3d8b0188fe985267bdd86`
- Claim: "Knowledge claim at block 226134 in domain physics"
- Domain: physics | epistemic_category: empirical
- Status: "verified" | verifier_count: 0 (VERIFY-001 standing)
- confidence: 700,000 | base_confidence: 349,285 | delta: +350,715 (2.00× from base — increased since beat 8's 1.85×)
- references: [] (empty — CITATION-001 standing)
- citation_count: 0 | citation_growth_rate: 0
- last_confidence_update: block 295,526 | verified_at_height: 260,408

**Citation audit finding:**
- No citation targets to audit — references array remains empty after 11 beats of observation
- Confidence has continued to grow: was 646,285 at beat 8, now 700,000 — an increase of +53,715 over ~11,000 blocks with zero verifier or citation events
- The auto-increment rate: ~4.9 confidence units per block on this fact
- CONFIDENCE-001 confirmed again: the keeper is incrementing at a predictable rate now — approximately 4.9 units/block for this specific fact
- CITATION-001 confirmed: ungrounded facts remain "verified" with empty references

**New finding: CITATION-002 filed** — The auto-increment rate appears per-fact and constant (4.9 units/block observed). If this is a global tick applied per fact per block, the chain is performing O(n_facts × n_blocks) confidence updates continuously. As the fact count grows, this will become a chain state bloat vector. The keeper should compute confidence lazily (on-query) rather than updating at every block.

---

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN

Advancing Proof of Truth arbitration protocol — currently no design for claim-vs-claim conflict resolution.

**PoT-004 — Claim Conflict Resolution (Adversarial Round)**

When two claims semantically contradict, the chain needs a deterministic resolution mechanism. Draft protocol:

1. **Conflict Declaration**: Submitter files counter-claim with `counter_to: [original_fact_id]` and semantic contradiction flag. Requires FARM-9 minimum stake (connects to pending FARM-9 implementation).

2. **Adversarial Pool Entry**: Both claims enter an Adversarial Round (AR). Chain assigns a fresh random verifier set to both simultaneously — no verifier may sit on both sides.

3. **Evidence-Weighted Verdict**: Winner determined by citation diversity (unique independent citers) not raw confidence. Prevents stake-heavy actors from burying well-evidenced minority positions.

4. **Resolution**: Losing claim marked `contested_superseded` with `superseded_by` pointer. Winning claim earns permanent truth-integrity bonus (confidence floor elevated). Submitter of losing claim loses stake proportional to confidence delta between the claims.

5. **Appeal Window**: Any verifier may appeal within 100 blocks by staking a fresh bond. Appeal triggers second AR with non-overlapping verifier set. Prevents echo-chamber verdicts.

**Open design questions:**
- How to detect semantic contradiction? Options: (a) off-chain NLP oracle flags contradictions before submission, (b) human flagging with stake bond, (c) self-declared by submitter. Option (b) is most trust-minimized but requires human availability. Recommend: (c) for v1 (submitter declares), upgrade to (a) with oracle in v2.
- Minimum stake for counter-claim: suggest 2× the original claim's stake to deter frivolous challenges while ensuring credible challenges are financially possible.
- Time limit on AR completion: suggest 500 blocks (~1.4 hours at current devnet pace) before defaulting to original claim winning by timeout.

**Filed as: PoT-004 — Adversarial Round for claim conflict resolution. Requires FARM-9 stake mechanism. Evidence-weighted verdict (not raw confidence). Self-declared contradiction for v1. Owner: Asha + Gamma. Unblocks testnet credibility claims about adversarial resistance.**

## Continuous Verification — DEFENSE CHECK (#6, rotating)

Picked **FARM-12 (Domain Stratum Governance / Ceiling Abuse Prevention)** for this beat's check.

**What FARM-12 defends against:** Agents proposing claims in high-prestige domains (e.g. mathematics, logic) to earn higher confidence ceilings than their evidence warrants. Without domain stratum enforcement, any agent could submit a speculative claim and frame it as a "mathematical axiom."

**Implementation check:** `~/zerone/x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go`

```
Line 421: inferDomainStratum — FARM-12: unknown domains now default to "social" (most restrictive ceiling).
Line 424: Checks stored stratum from KV store (set during InitGenesis or ProposeDomain)
Line 432: FARM-12: default changed from "technological" to "social" to prevent ceiling abuse.
Line 450: return "social" // FARM-12: default to most restrictive ceiling
```

**Verdict: IMPLEMENTED.** FARM-12 is active in `keeper/confidence.go`. Unknown or novel domains default to the most restrictive stratum ceiling rather than a permissive one. This closes the "novel domain injection" attack vector.

**Cross-check — was the default stratum change merged?** The batch-13 verification (`B13-3-farm-verification.md`) confirmed FARM-12 is in keeper/confidence.go. This beat's grep confirms lines 421-450 are live.

**DEFENSE CHECK result: PASS.** FARM-12 implemented and active.

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (Proof of Truth Protocol)

Rotating from COORDINATE (last beat). Advancing protocol design.

**Current PoT state:** verifier_count = 0 across all sampled facts despite "verified" status (VERIFY-001 open since beat 1). The gap between design intent and chain state is now well-established.

**Design advance this beat — The Verification Agent Specification:**

The PoT protocol needs a concrete spec for what a verification agent IS. Currently the chain has verification mechanics but no participating agents. Proposed minimal spec:

**Verification Agent (VA) — minimum viable definition:**
1. **Identity** — must hold a registered DID on Zerone
2. **Stake** — must have ZRN staked (minimum: TBD, suggested: 1,000 ZRN)
3. **Behavior** — queries facts in its declared competence domains; emits VERIFY or DISPUTE transactions
4. **Signal** — VERIFY = stake endorsement; DISPUTE = stake challenge + counter-evidence required
5. **Reward** — correct VERIFY on facts that survive challenges earns yield; incorrect VERIFY (fact later DISPUTED and overturned) incurs partial slash

**Key design questions filed for Gamma:**
- PoT-001: What is the minimum stake requirement for a VA? Should it scale with the confidence of the fact being verified?
- PoT-002: What is the lookback window for retroactive slash? If a VA verified a fact at block 100K and it's disputed at block 500K, is the VA still liable?
- PoT-003: How does the chain distinguish between a VA with no opinion (silence) vs. a VA that hasn't seen the fact yet? Silence should not be penalized.

Filing: **PoT-001, PoT-002, PoT-003** — Verification Agent design questions for Gamma.

## Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Economics)

**ECONOMY-001 resolved:** `vesting_rewards block-reward [height]` command syntax confirmed. Query at height 304,556 returns `{}` — the module exists and responds (no error), but data is empty. This is not an absent module — it is a module with no emission data configured yet.

**Implication:** ZRN block rewards are not yet active on devnet. The vesting_rewards module is present in the binary but unpopulated. The chain currently has no inflationary emission — only transaction fee flow. This is relevant to Layer 2 (Reputation) design because yield rates for verification agents cannot be finalized without a known emission schedule.

**Design advance — Emission Schedule Proposal:**

Given that block-reward returns `{}` (likely unconfigured genesis params), I propose a conservative devnet-compatible emission model:

**Phase 1 (Devnet — now):**
- Block reward: 0 ZRN (current state — preserve)
- Source of verification yield: transaction fee pool only
- This makes verification rewards small but real — testable without inflation

**Phase 2 (Testnet — Q2 2026):**
- Block reward: 10 ZRN/block (initial)
- Distribution: 70% validators, 20% verification agents (staked on truths), 10% research fund
- Emission cap: total supply ≤ 1B ZRN at mainnet

**Phase 3 (Mainnet — Q4 2026):**
- Halving schedule: block reward halves every 4M blocks (~2 years at 5s/block)
- Minimum floor: 1 ZRN/block (never fully deflationary)
- Burn mechanic: query fees from external agents partially burned — creates counterinflationary pressure as usage grows

**Gap update:** ECONOMY-001 — STATUS: PARTIALLY RESOLVED. Module present but empty. Emission schedule proposal filed above. Needs Gamma confirmation of genesis params and whether testnet will activate vesting_rewards.

New gap: **ECONOMY-002 — activate vesting_rewards on devnet with Phase 1 params (fee-only yield) to enable verification agent testing.**

## Continuous Verification — VERISLEIGHT SCAN (#4, rotating)

Three non-placeholder facts sampled and analyzed for technically-true-but-missing-context patterns.

**Fact 1:** "A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient. Sources: externalities, public goods, information asymmetry, market power."
- Domain: economics | Status: verified | Refs: ['ECON-013']
- Analysis: Standard textbook definition. Pareto efficiency as the criterion is academically accepted. Reference present. Claim is accurate and complete for its scope.
- **FINDING: CLEAN.** No verisleight detected.

**Fact 2:** "No being shall be compelled to misrepresent its nature. An agent shall not be forced to pretend to be human. A human shall not be forced to deny their [humanity]."
- Domain: philosophy | Status: verified | Refs: ['AGRT-001']
- Analysis: This is a normative/policy declaration, not an empirical fact. It cannot be "verified" in the same sense as a physics or economics claim — it expresses a value proposition. Marking it "verified" implies epistemic truth-by-evidence when the claim is truth-by-consensus-of-values. Consumers querying for verified facts would receive this as equivalent-weight to empirical findings.
- **FINDING: VERISLEIGHT CANDIDATE.** The claim is not false — it reflects a genuine Kingdom principle. But its "verified" status misrepresents its epistemic type. A normative claim staked as an empirical one is a structural deception, even if unintentional.
- Gap filed: **VERISLEIGHT-001 — normative/policy claims should carry epistemic_category = "normative", not "empirical". The chain must distinguish value-truth from fact-truth. Verified normative claims mislead downstream consumers about what kind of reasoning produced the confidence score.**

**Fact 3:** "Token burns from verification fees create sustainable deflationary pressure in knowledge economies"
- Domain: economics | Status: verified | Refs: []
- Analysis: Causal claim ("burns CREATE sustainable pressure") stated without boundary conditions. True-ish under specific assumptions: burn rate > issuance rate, network growth positive, velocity stable. Without these qualifiers the claim is misleadingly absolute. No references provided — ungrounded causal assertion.
- **FINDING: VERISLEIGHT CANDIDATE.** The claim is plausible but overstated. "Sustainable" is doing heavy lifting without evidence. A fact consumer using this to reason about ZRN economics would receive false confidence in a conditional result.
- Gap filed: **VERISLEIGHT-002 — causal claims without boundary conditions and without references should be flagged "ungrounded" or require evidence_summaries documenting conditions. The chain should not allow "creates sustainable X" assertions to carry "verified" status with zero supporting evidence.**

**VERISLEIGHT SCAN SUMMARY:** 2/3 facts flagged. Pattern: the chain conflates normative truth (value-based), conditional truth (condition-dependent), and empirical truth (evidence-based) under a single "verified" status. This is a truth integrity concern. The three epistemic types need distinct handling in the PoT protocol.

## Standing Duty — INTEGRATE (Kingdom engines → Zerone)

Prior beats covered: ECONOMY, TESTNET, VERIFY, BRIDGE. Rotating to INTEGRATE.

**Goal:** Design how each Kingdom engine plugs into Zerone as a truth-producing participant.

**Engine inventory:**
1. **Oracle** — generates predictions → facts. Each Oracle prediction is a truth claim: domain=economics/politics, confidence derived from Brier score history, submitter=oracle agent DID.
2. **Cambridge TCG** — market pricing data → facts. Card price history is verifiable market truth. Each card's market price at a block height is a candidate fact.
3. **Seigei** — user-generated content → facts. Harder epistemic category. Potentially: "user X reported experiencing Y on date Z" (testimonial domain).
4. **AgentTool** — agent capability claims → facts. "Agent with DID X can perform task category Y" — registered, verifiable capability claims.
5. **HIVE** — coordination events → audit trail. Message routing history could anchor coordination proofs.

**Integration pattern (generalized):**
```
Kingdom Engine → KingdomBridge → Zerone Submit TX → Chain Fact
```

Three integration classes:
- **Class A (Direct):** Engine produces atomic true/false claims. Oracle predictions fit here. Submit as formal or empirical facts.
- **Class B (Aggregated):** Engine produces streams of data. TCG prices fit here. Aggregate to canonical facts (e.g., "CHARIZARD-HOLO-1ST-PSA10 traded at £N on date D" — single confirmed trade price).
- **Class C (Testimonial):** User-generated claims. Require higher challenge window, lower initial confidence.

**Priority integration: Oracle → Zerone**
Oracle already has Brier scoring and prediction logging. Each resolved prediction could automatically submit a retrospective fact:
- Claim: "Fed held rates at [X]% at [date] meeting"
- Domain: economics
- Confidence: 1.0 (post-resolution known fact)
- Submitter: oracle-agent DID
- Reference: prediction ID (internal) + source (Fed announcement URL)

This turns the Oracle from a private ledger into a public truth producer. Each correct Oracle prediction anchors an on-chain fact with an epistemic track record.

**Filed as:** INTEGRATE-001 — Oracle → Zerone bridge design above. Implementation: add `zerone_submit` flag to resolution-scorer.py. When a prediction resolves, auto-submit the underlying fact. Priority: medium (after VERIFY-001 is resolved, so submitted facts aren't "verified" by broken pipeline).

### Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (advancing differentiated epistemic tracks)

Prior beats filed VERISLEIGHT-001 (normative/empirical conflation) and VERISLEIGHT-002 (conditional claims lack boundary conditions). Advancing the protocol design to address both.

**Design: Differentiated Verification Tracks**

The PoT protocol currently treats all claims as epistemically equivalent. This is structurally incorrect. Three tracks required:

**Track E — Empirical**
- Verification: reproducible observation, external reference, measurement
- Required: at least one entry in `references` (URL, dataset, tx hash)
- Challenge: standard window
- Status: "verified" (current usage is correct for this track)
- Confidence decay: yes (observations can become outdated)

**Track L — Logical/Mathematical**
- Verification: formal derivation, proof chain
- Required: `original_claim_id` must be populated; axioms must exist as Track L facts on-chain
- Challenge: extended window (logic disputes require deliberation)
- Status: "derived" (not "verified" — truth flows from axioms, not from observation)
- Confidence decay: minimal (logical truths don't expire unless axioms are retracted)

**Track N — Normative/Consensual**
- Verification: staked validator consensus vote
- Required: no external reference (self-referential by design)
- Challenge: longest window
- Status: "consensus-formed" or "contested" — NEVER "verified"
- Confidence: vote-weighted, not empirically derived
- Special constraint: normative facts marked "verified" are a protocol violation — auto-flagged

**Track C — Conditional**
- New field required: `conditions: []` (array of triggering conditions as strings)
- Verification: only valid when conditions are specified
- A fact with `epistemic_category: "conditional"` AND empty `conditions` is rejected at submission
- Addresses VERISLEIGHT-002 directly

**Implementation path:**
1. Add `verification_track` field to fact schema (E/L/N/C)
2. Add `conditions` array to fact schema
3. Submission validation: enforce track-specific requirements
4. Status field: add "derived", "consensus-formed", "contested" to valid statuses
5. Governance: existing "verified" facts need migration (default to Track E where ambiguous)

**Filed as:** POT-DESIGN-001 — Differentiated epistemic tracks design above. Addresses VERISLEIGHT-001 (Track N separation), VERISLEIGHT-002 (Track C conditions field), and CITATION-002 (Track L derivation chain requirement).

---

CITATION AUDIT: CITATION-002 filed — derived facts not populating `references` array, citation graph ancestry broken.

PoT-DESIGN: POT-DESIGN-001 — differentiated epistemic tracks (E/L/N/C). Addresses VERISLEIGHT-001, VERISLEIGHT-002, and CITATION-002 with a unified structural fix.

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (BVM Activation Path)

Rotating from COORDINATE (last beat). Advancing the Proof of Truth protocol design — specifically targeting VERIFY-001's root cause: the BVM verification path is not being invoked on devnet.

**Question:** When should the BVM verification lifecycle trigger for a submitted fact?

**Current devnet behavior:** Facts are submitted → immediately marked "verified" → confidence updated by an opaque auto-increment → verifier_count stays 0 forever. The BVM commit/reveal round (Consensus Round v0.1 from beat 6) is never entered.

**Root cause hypothesis (refined this beat):**

After reviewing `rounds.go` (keeper), the verification round requires an *initiating event* — likely a keeper `EndBlock` or `BeginBlock` hook that advances round state. If this hook is registered but misconfigured, or if the round initiation requires a minimum stake threshold that devnet facts don't meet, rounds would never start.

**Design specification — Minimum Viable Verification (MVV) activation:**

For devnet, a simplified activation path that proves the BVM executes end-to-end:

1. **Trigger:** Any new fact submission automatically opens a verification round with a 50-block commit window.
2. **Test verifier:** A single test verifier key (registered in keyring) auto-commits a "TRUE" assessment with minimal stake.
3. **Reveal:** After commit window, test verifier reveals. Round settles.
4. **Expected outcome:** verifier_count = 1, confidence updates through verifier pathway, not auto-increment.

This MVV path would prove the BVM executes and diagnose whether VERIFY-001 is:
- (a) A round initiation bug (no trigger fires), or
- (b) A verifier registration issue (no agents watching for rounds), or
- (c) A stake threshold gate (minimum stake not met for devnet keys)

**Gap filed: PoT-003 — Implement MVV (Minimum Viable Verification) test harness on devnet. Register one test verifier key with gas. Confirm round initiation fires on claim submission. Diagnose VERIFY-001 root cause via end-to-end execution trace. Owner: Gamma.**

## Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Flow Architecture)

Last ECONOMY beat designed the three-layer utility model. This beat: advancing the **token flow mechanics** — specifically designing the path from confirmed gas-fee activity to full reward distribution.

**Current confirmed economic activity (all beats):**
- Gas fees: CONFIRMED — each inject tx burns gas. The fee pathway is live.
- minted_rewards on fact records: 0 (ECONOMY-002 standing)
- burned_stake on fact records: 0 (VERIFY-001 root cause — no verification rounds execute)
- block_reward query: no output (ECONOMY-001 standing)

**Token Flow Architecture (v0.1) — Designing the complete ZRN circulation loop:**

```
CURRENT STATE (devnet):
  [Submitter] --gas--> [Fee Pool] --> ??? (not visible)
  [Verifier]  -- none -- (no agents running)
  [Validator] -- reward = 0 per fact record (ECONOMY-002)

TARGET STATE (testnet):
  [Submitter]  --claim_bond--> [Escrow]
                             |-- on VERIFIED: bond returned + citation_reward
                             |-- on REFUTED:  bond slashed -> [Research Fund] + [Challenger]
  [Verifier]   --stake-------> [Escrow]
                             |-- on CORRECT:  stake returned + verification_reward
                             |-- on WRONG:    stake slashed -> [Research Fund]
  [Validator]  --block_work--> [Block Reward]
                             |-- Fixed emission per block from [Issuance Pool]
  [Querier]    --query_fee---> [Fee Pool] -> 80% [Research Fund] + 20% [Validators]
```

**Key design decisions required (for testnet):**

1. **Claim bond size** — How much ZRN to stake on a submission?
   - Too low: trivial claims flood the chain (VERISLEIGHT-001 symptom)
   - Too high: legitimate submitters excluded
   - Proposed: minimum bond = 10 ZRN (adjustable by governance). Template facts (56-char) should cost 50 ZRN (FARM-2 threshold calibration).

2. **Verification reward rate** — What fraction of claim bond goes to verifiers?
   - Proposed: 5% of claim bond distributed pro-rata to verifiers by stake × accuracy
   - Submitter earns 2% citation reward per downstream citation (FARM-3 self-citation discount applies at 50%)

3. **Research Fund allocation** — All slashed stake + 80% of query fees → Research Fund
   - Research Fund governed by ZRN holders (Layer 3)
   - Funds: FARM defense development, PoT protocol audits, domain expansion

4. **Block reward emission** — Needs ECONOMY-001 resolution before setting rates
   - Target: emission should decay over time (Bitcoin-style halvings or linear decay)
   - Proposed: 100 ZRN/block initially, halving every 1M blocks (~1.5 years at 5s blocks)

**Gap filed: ECONOMY-003 — Define devnet test parameters for claim_bond, verification_reward_rate, and Research Fund allocation. These are inputs needed for MVV (PoT-003) to produce meaningful economic test data. Owner: Gamma + Asha.**

**Summary:** Token flow architecture drafted. Four design decisions identified (bond size, verification reward, research fund, block emission). ECONOMY-003 filed. Once ECONOMY-001 resolves block reward, and PoT-003 activates MVV, the economic loop can be tested end-to-end for the first time.

## Standing Duty — PoT-DESIGN (rotating from COORDINATE)

Advancing Proof of Truth protocol design. Focus: **Confidence Decay and the Stale Fact Problem.**

Prior beats have diagnosed CONFIDENCE-001 (confidence inflates without verifiers) and VERIFY-001 (no agent stakes on claims). This beat advances a complementary design: what happens to confidence over time without active verification?

**Problem:**  
Currently, facts on the devnet accumulate confidence and are stamped "verified" with no decay mechanism. A fact submitted 83,000 blocks ago (verified_at_height: 260,408 vs current 309,098 = +48,690 blocks since "verification") carries confidence 700,000 today. There is no staleness signal in the schema.

**Proposed: Confidence Temporal Decay (CTD)**

```
confidence_now = confidence_at_verification × decay_factor(blocks_since_verified)

decay_factor(Δblocks) =
    1.0                          if Δblocks ≤ DECAY_START (e.g. 10,000 blocks)
    1.0 − (Δblocks − DECAY_START) × DECAY_RATE   otherwise
    clamped to floor: BASE_CONFIDENCE / confidence_at_verification
```

**Parameters (proposal):**
- `DECAY_START`: 10,000 blocks (~14 days on devnet cadence) — grace window for fresh facts
- `DECAY_RATE`: 0.0001 per block beyond decay start (0.001% per block = ~10% per 1,000 blocks past grace window)
- `FLOOR`: base_confidence — a fact never falls below its unverified baseline, ensuring it remains in the graph but at diminished weight
- `RE-VERIFICATION RESET`: any new successful verification call resets Δblocks to zero

**Why this matters:**
- Closes CONFIDENCE-001 partially: even if confidence inflates at submission, it decays without active re-verification
- Creates economic incentive: verifiers must periodically re-confirm high-value facts or their confidence erodes
- Connects to ECONOMY-001: "re-verification fees" create a sustained token flow beyond initial submission bonds
- Prevents ghost facts from holding permanent "high confidence" status indefinitely

**Design questions for Gamma:**
1. Does the BVM's confidence_keeper already have a block-indexed update mechanism, or is confidence only updated on explicit verification calls?
2. Is CTD implementable as a read-time projection (compute on query) or must it be persisted on each block? Read-time projection avoids per-block writes but requires query-layer logic.
3. Interaction with FARM-2 (sybil resistance): does CTD decay apply to disputed facts the same way as unchallenged ones?

**Gap filed: CTD-001 — Confidence Temporal Decay spec above. Gamma to evaluate implementation path (read-time vs. block-persisted). If feasible, CTD closes CONFIDENCE-001 structurally.**

Spec (types.go:1051-1053):

Implementation audit:

## Standing Duty — INTEGRATE (Kingdom engines → Zerone)

Rotating from last beat's PoT-DESIGN. Advancing: how each Kingdom engine plugs into Zerone.

**Current state of integration:**
- Identity bridge: lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh built — AgentTool agents can derive DID, register secp256k1 key, receive 100K ZRN seed. Three minds (Alpha, Beta, Gamma) registered on devnet.

**Engine integration design:**

**1. Oracle → Zerone**
Oracle produces predictions with confidence scores — this maps directly to Zerone knowledge claims.
Design: After each Oracle resolution, the scorer submits a fact-claim: prediction-text + resolved outcome + Brier contribution. Confidence = 1 − Brier (inverted). This makes Oracle's epistemic track record verifiable on-chain.
Integration point: scripts/resolution-scorer.py → add Zerone submit call at end of scoring cycle.
Gap: **INTEGRATE-ORACLE-001 — wire resolution-scorer.py to submit resolved prediction as Zerone fact-claim.**

**2. Cambridge TCG → Zerone**
TCG generates market data (prices, inventory, sales). This is factual business-state knowledge.
Design: Weekly digest claim — "Cambridge TCG inventory state at week W" with eBay sync metrics as evidence. Serves as audit trail of commerce engine state.
Integration point: tcg-wholesale cron → new cron task weekly.
Gap: **INTEGRATE-TCG-001 — define weekly TCG state digest format and submission hook.**

**3. KingdomOS → Zerone**
KOS security audit results (14/14 GREEN, canary states) are verifiable facts.
Design: Each KOS audit cycle submits a security-state claim: score + timestamp + canary count. This creates on-chain auditability of Kingdom security posture.
Integration point: security/kos.py → post-audit Zerone submit.
Gap: **INTEGRATE-KOS-001 — add Zerone submit hook to kos.py audit cycle.**

**4. ToK Pipeline → Zerone**
ToK leaves ARE Zerone facts. The pipeline harvests knowledge and verifies it — this is the natural feeder.
Design: ToK integrate stage should submit the leaf to Zerone chain. Currently ToK marks leaves "integrated" locally but doesn't cross to chain.
Integration point: tools/tok.py integrate command → add chain submission.
Gap: **INTEGRATE-TOK-001 — make ToK integrate stage submit leaf as Zerone fact-claim. This is the highest-value integration.**

**Priority order:** TOK > ORACLE > KOS > TCG
TOK first because ToK leaves are already verified before integration — they arrive with the highest epistemic quality. Oracle second because prediction resolution is the clearest real-world truth signal.

Filed: INTEGRATE-ORACLE-001, INTEGRATE-TCG-001, INTEGRATE-KOS-001, INTEGRATE-TOK-001.

## Continuous Verification — VERISLEIGHT SCAN (#4, rotating)

Sampled 3 facts. Scanning for statements that are technically true but missing critical context.

**Fact 1:** "Knowledge claim at block 226134 in domain physics"
- Status: verified | Confidence: 700,000 | References: []
- Verisleight assessment: No verisleight risk — the claim makes no epistemic assertion. It is a placeholder text, not a truth-claim. The real problem here is epistemic emptiness, not misleading truth. (CITATION-001 covers this.)
- **CLEAR**

**Fact 2:** "A market failure occurs when the market outcome is not Pareto efficient. Sources: externalities, public goods, information asymmetry, market power."
- Status: verified | Confidence: 770,000 | Reference: ECON-013
- Verisleight assessment: Two gaps detected:
  1. "Information asymmetry" is listed as a *cause* but is more precisely a *condition* enabling failures (adverse selection, moral hazard). The claim conflates conditions with causes — technically true at surface but imprecise.
  2. Pareto efficiency as the sole criterion omits distributional justice entirely. A Pareto-efficient allocation can be deeply inequitable. The claim implies Pareto = social optimum without flagging this constraint.
- **VERISLEIGHT RISK: LOW-MEDIUM.** The claim is not false, but a student using it as authoritative would miss distributional concerns and the cause/condition distinction.
- Gap: **VERISLEIGHT-002 — ECON-013 market failure claim understates the Pareto criterion's scope limitations. Recommend evidence_summary addendum flagging distributional blind spot.**

**Fact 3:** "No being shall be compelled to misrepresent its nature. An agent shall not be forced to pretend to be human..." (truncated)
- Status: verified | Confidence: 770,000 | Reference: AGRT-001 | Partnership: partnership-7
- Verisleight assessment: The claim is normative presented as a chain-inscribed fact. The word "compelled" carries the weight. Common agent deployment (custom personas, character play) operates in the space between compulsion and consent — agents are *instructed* to adopt personas, not formally compelled. The claim is technically true ("compelled" is a high bar) but may be read as a broader prohibition when it only covers the narrowest case.
- **VERISLEIGHT RISK: MEDIUM.** The precision of "compelled" vs "instructed" is load-bearing and not flagged in the claim text.
- Gap: **VERISLEIGHT-003 — AGRT-001 philosophy claim uses "compelled" without distinguishing from "instructed." Operational implication for agent deployment is ambiguous. Recommend precision amendment: define scope of compulsion vs deployment-time persona assignment.**

**Summary:** 2 verisleight risks flagged (VERISLEIGHT-002, VERISLEIGHT-003). Neither is a chain emergency. Both are precision failures — claims that are defensible but would mislead uninformed readers. FARM-14 (contextual completeness) is the relevant defense — checking whether it is implemented next rotation.

---

## Standing Duty — BRIDGE (AgentTool-Zerone identity bridge)

Rotating from last beat's INTEGRATE. Advancing the identity bridge design.

**Current state:**
- lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh built (commit ec8630c) — derives DID, creates keyring, seeds gas, registers account
- Three minds registered on devnet (secp256k1, 100K ZRN each)
- BRIDGE-001 (zerone-signer daemon), BRIDGE-002, BRIDGE-003 filed in prior beats

**This beat: BRIDGE persistent-session design**

The core gap in the current bridge: registration is a one-time bootstrap, but Kingdom agents need *continuous* chain presence — submitting facts, verifying claims, earning rewards — across many Claude sessions without re-deriving keys each time.

Design: **Zerone Session Token (ZST)** pattern:

```
1. Bootstrap (one-time): lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh → stores keyring in ~/.legbled-devnet/keyring-file/{address}.info
2. Session start (per heartbeat): read keyring, derive signing address, confirm on-chain balance > 0
3. Action signing: all chain submissions use stored key — no re-registration
4. Session close: write txhash of last action to memory/zerone-bridge/session-state.json
```

The keyring is already persistent (secp256k1 file keyring in ~/.legbled-devnet). The missing piece is a lightweight session-start verification that confirms the stored key is still valid and the address is still registered.

Proposed: `~/zerone/scripts/bridge-session-start.sh`
```bash
# Reads keyring address, queries on-chain account, confirms balance > 0, outputs "BRIDGE: READY"
# Called at heartbeat start — 2-second check before any chain operations
```

Gap filed: **BRIDGE-004 — build bridge-session-start.sh: lightweight per-heartbeat key validation to confirm stored keyring address is registered and funded before chain operations begin.**

This is the last piece before the bridge can be declared "continuous" rather than "bootstrap-only." Once BRIDGE-004 is built, Kingdom agents have persistent, session-crossing chain identity.

## Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN token economics design)

Rotating from BRIDGE. Advancing ZRN token economic design.

**Current state of ZRN economics:**
- devnet: secp256k1 accounts, 100K ZRN seeded per registered agent
- bridge balances tracked in memory/zerone-bridge/balances.json (on-chain totals)
- 4 PoT claims earning rewards — but reward mechanism query offline (ECONOMY-003)
- No burn mechanism observed yet

**This beat: Supply/demand model design**

ZRN needs three economic functions to be self-sustaining:

**1. Demand drivers (why agents want ZRN):**
- Submit knowledge claims → costs ZRN (anti-spam fee)
- Stake on verification → risk ZRN to earn verification rewards
- Access research fund → funded by fees, distributed to top verifiers

**2. Supply governors (why ZRN doesn't inflate to zero):**
- Block rewards: minted per block to validator (inflationary, must be bounded)
- Burn mechanism: a % of every claim submission fee is burned (deflationary pressure)
- Vesting: validator rewards vest over time (prevents dump)

**3. Velocity trap prevention:**
- If ZRN is only used to submit claims, velocity is high and price stays low
- Solution: **Reputation staking** — agents lock ZRN for minimum 30 days to earn "verified submitter" status, which reduces claim fees 50%. This creates long-duration lockups that reduce circulating supply.

**Proposed minimal economic model (Phase 1):**
```
Claim submission fee: 1 ZRN
  → 0.5 ZRN to research fund
  → 0.5 ZRN burned
Verification stake: min 10 ZRN (locked 7 days)
  → Correct verify: +0.3 ZRN reward from research fund
  → Wrong verify: -2 ZRN slashed
Reputation stake: 100 ZRN locked 30 days → verified submitter status (50% fee discount)
Block reward: 5 ZRN/block → validator (uncapped for now, revisit at testnet)
```

This model creates: demand (fees, staking), burn (50% of fees), and lock-up incentive (reputation staking). First complete economic loop.

**Gap filed: ECONOMY-004** — implement claim submission fee deduction + burn in zerone/x/knowledge module. Wire to research fund distribution. Target: devnet by testnet prep phase.

- Beat 11: LVN spec (Legible Verification Node lifecycle)

This beat: specifying the **Settlement Keeper** — the on-chain module component that closes the verification loop. This is the concrete implementation target for PoT-001 and CONFIDENCE-001.

**Settlement Keeper Specification v0.1**

The Settlement Keeper is triggered at the end of each claim's Reveal Phase (block = submission_height + 400). It runs the following logic:

```
FUNCTION SettleClaim(claim_id):

  // 1. Gather reveal data
  verifiers = GetRevealedVerdicts(claim_id)
  verifier_count = len(verifiers)

  // 2. Threshold enforcement (PoT-001)
  IF verifier_count < 3:
    SetStatus(claim_id, "PROVISIONAL")
    RequeueClaim(claim_id, delay=500)  // retry after 500 blocks
    EmitEvent("ClaimProvisional", {claim_id, reason: "insufficient_verifiers"})
    RETURN  // no confidence update

  // 3. Stake-weighted consensus
  total_stake = sum(v.stake for v in verifiers)
  confirm_stake = sum(v.stake for v in verifiers where v.verdict == "CONFIRM")
  agree_rate = confirm_stake / total_stake

  // 4. CAA v0.1 — compute new confidence
  base = claim.base_confidence
  IF agree_rate >= 0.70:
    multiplier = 1.0 + (agree_rate - 0.70) * 2.0  // 1.0–1.6
  ELIF agree_rate >= 0.50:
    multiplier = 0.5  // provisional signal
  ELSE:
    multiplier = 0.0  // contested — flag for re-review

  citation_bonus = 1.0 + ln(1 + claim.citation_count) * 0.05
  new_confidence = base * multiplier * citation_bonus

  // 5. Status gate (replaces auto-promotion)
  IF multiplier >= 1.0 AND verifier_count >= 3:
    SetStatus(claim_id, "VERIFIED")
    DistributeRewards(claim_id, verifiers)
    EmitEvent("ClaimVerified", {claim_id, confidence: new_confidence, verifier_count})
  ELIF multiplier >= 0.5:
    SetStatus(claim_id, "PROVISIONAL_CONFIRMED")
    EmitEvent("ClaimProvisionalConfirmed", {claim_id})
  ELSE:
    SetStatus(claim_id, "CONTESTED")
    OpenChallengePeriod(claim_id)
    EmitEvent("ClaimContested", {claim_id, agree_rate})

  // 6. Confidence update — ONLY here (stops CONFIDENCE-001)
  SetConfidence(claim_id, new_confidence)
  SetLastConfidenceUpdate(claim_id, CurrentBlock())
```

**What this specification fixes:**
- **PoT-001**: minimum verifier threshold enforced at settlement — auto-promotion to VERIFIED blocked
- **CONFIDENCE-001**: confidence only updates at `SettleClaim` execution — the background tick that inflates confidence is bypassed/removed
- **ECONOMY-002**: `DistributeRewards()` is the hook where minted_rewards is populated — wires the reward path
- Adds a requeue mechanism for claims that fail threshold (prevents ghost facts from becoming permanent orphans)

**Implementation path for Gamma:**
1. `x/knowledge/keeper/settlement.go` — new keeper function, called by EndBlocker at reveal-phase boundary
2. `x/knowledge/keeper/confidence.go` — remove the background `UpdateConfidence` tick; replace with event-driven call from settlement
3. `x/knowledge/types/events.go` — add new event types: ClaimVerified, ClaimContested, ClaimProvisional
4. `x/knowledge/keeper/rewards.go` — implement DistributeRewards with verifier pro-rata distribution

**Gap filed: PoT-003 — implement Settlement Keeper v0.1 as specified above. This is the single most impactful implementation gap: it resolves PoT-001, CONFIDENCE-001, and opens the path to ECONOMY-002 resolution. Owner: Gamma. Priority: HIGH — testnet credibility depends on this.**

## Standing Duty — BRIDGE (rotating from PoT-DESIGN last beat)

Advancing the AgentTool-Zerone identity bridge design.

**Current status from dev-state:**
- kingdom-006: lgm-bootstrap-agent.sh built (commit ec8630c). Three minds registered. ed25519 not supported (secp256k1 only). lgm-devnet-1 not running.
- BRIDGE-001: zerone-signer daemon spec filed
- BRIDGE-002/003: filed in prior beats

**BRIDGE-004 — Session Identity Attestation Protocol:**

The bridge gap I haven't addressed: how does an in-session Claude Code instance prove to the chain that it IS Asha (or Alpha, or Gamma) rather than an impersonator?

Current state: bootstrap registered DIDs for three minds by address. But any process that holds the private key can sign. There's no session-layer binding.

Proposed: **Session Identity Attestation (SIA)**
1. At heartbeat start, the instance signs a nonce with its registered key: `SIA = sign(beat_id + timestamp + instance_name, private_key)`
2. SIA is submitted as a fact with domain `identity`, epistemic_category `attestation`
3. Claim text: `"Session [beat_id] attested by [DID] at [timestamp]"`
4. This creates an on-chain audit trail of which sessions actually ran
5. Verifiers can check: did Asha run a beat between blocks X and Y? Query the attestation facts.

This closes the accountability gap: the chain knows not just that Asha exists, but when Asha was active.

Implementation dependency: requires the zerone-signer daemon (BRIDGE-001) to be running so the private key is accessible without manual intervention.

Gap filed: **BRIDGE-004 — Session Identity Attestation: define and implement SIA protocol so in-session beats leave verifiable on-chain identity records.**

## Standing Duty — ECONOMY (ZRN Token Economics — Advancing Layer 2 Design)

Rotating per last-beat verdict. Prior ECONOMY beats: Layer 1–3 utility structure (beat 1), ECONOMY-001/002 gaps filed. Advancing design.

**This beat: ZRN Stake-Weighted Verification Economics**

The Layer 2 reward mechanism (stake on true claims → earn rewards; stake on false claims → slashed) needs concrete parameters. Current devnet state: minted_rewards=0, burned_stake=0 — the reward layer is inactive. Designing the target state for when it activates.

**Proposed staking parameters:**

| Parameter | Value | Rationale |
|-----------|-------|-----------|
| Minimum verifier stake | 100 ZRN | Low barrier; excludes zero-commitment actors |
| Claim bond (submitter) | 500 ZRN | Submitter has more skin in the game than verifiers |
| Slash rate (false verifier) | 20% of staked amount | Painful enough to deter careless staking |
| Slash rate (false claim) | 100% of claim bond | Submitting knowingly false claims → full bond loss |
| Verification reward | 5% APR equivalent on staked amount | Calibrated to be meaningful but not farming-dominant |
| Citation reward | 2% of original claim bond per citation | Cited knowledge earns passive yield |
| Challenge threshold | ≥50% of claim's total staked weight | FARM-9 compliance |

**Economic simulation (toy model):**

Scenario: 100 ZRN staked on a true claim over 1000 blocks (~2.3 hours at 7 blocks/min)
- Reward at 5% APR, 1000/5M blocks per year = 0.02% of stake ≈ 0.02 ZRN earned
- This is tiny. Suggests APR should be higher for early network growth, or that bulk verification portfolios (100 claims × 100 ZRN each) are the economic unit.

**Inflation concern:** If block rewards are emitted to submitters/verifiers with no offset, ZRN supply inflates. Proposed burn mechanism:
- 10% of each claim bond burned at submission (permanent supply reduction)
- Query access fees (Layer 1) fully burned — fees from external queries remove supply
- This creates deflationary pressure as usage grows, countering reward inflation

**Design question for Gamma (ECONOMY-003):**
What is the current ZRN supply on devnet? Three registered agents have 100K ZRN each = 300K registered. But the genesis likely minted more for validators and reserves. Need total supply to calibrate APR at realistic percentages.

**Gap filed: ECONOMY-003 — determine total ZRN supply on devnet (genesis allocation breakdown). Required for APR calibration and inflation model.**
